# Build 216 capability inventory (AnxHost CC)

Generated by `node scripts/anxhost-capability-inventory.js` from the shipped UI sources (`index.html`, `app.js`, `src/renderer/*.js`, `main.js`, `preload.js`, `src/mobile/web/*`, `src/mobile/api.js`, `src/shared/serverViewModel.js`). Do not edit rows by hand; edit the generator or the UI and regenerate. Ticks in the first column are kept when the file is regenerated.

`npm run anxhost-capability-inventory:smoke` fails when a row is unmapped or when the UI and this inventory disagree (a hook was removed or added without regenerating). During 216-A every wave keeps its rows working, ticks them here after verification, and regenerates deliberately when a hook changes.

## Columns

- **✓**: ticked once the row is verified working in its Build 216 destination.
- **Kind**: page, nav (sidebar target), tab (tab or section switch), action, input, control (button or link without an action attribute), dialog, shortcut, window, ipc (preload method), template (Marketplace catalog entry; each needs bundled artwork in A4), phone-screen, phone-tab, phone-api.
- **Hook**: the stable selector (`data-*` attribute and value), function, IPC method or route that identifies the capability. `*` means the value is computed at runtime.
- **Today**: file and line in Build 215 (`13825d70`); `×n` when the hook appears n times in that area.
- **Backend / IPC**: for ipc rows, the IPC channel and the UI files that call it; for phone-api rows, the phone UI caller. UI rows reach the backend through the ipc rows of the same area.
- **Tests**: test scripts in `scripts/` and `test-helpers/` that reference the hook (first four, then the total).

## IPC notes

Preload exposes 447 methods (`window.anx` and its aliases, plus `window.anxWindow`). UI callers were found by method name: 142, by method name near its namespace: 45, exact: 213, none: 47. "By method name" covers the common pattern of aliasing a namespace first (`const api = ...instances; api.safeRestart()`).

47 methods have no direct call in the renderer. Each is classified by the evidence the renderer does contain, stays mapped to the area that owns it, and is confirmed by behaviour in its wave (the backend is unchanged in every case):

| Evidence | Count | Methods (wave, where) |
| --- | --- | --- |
| feature-detected with typeof | 15 | `account.getStatus` (A1, app.js:2009), `anxWindow.isMaximized` (A1, app.js:1944), `anxWindow.onMaximizedChanged` (A1, app.js:1945), `window.isMaximized` (A1, app.js:1944), `window.onMaximizedChanged` (A1, app.js:1945), `window.openWorkspace` (A1, app.js:1898), `telemetry.getSummary` (A2, app.js:41505), `actions.executeAction` (A3, app.js:2032), `instances.getFiveMReadiness` (A3, app.js:2030), `instances.mods.setEnabled` (A3, app.js:44038), `dependencies.getCatalog` (A4, app.js:1987), `marketplace.getProviderPackDetails` (A4, app.js:1977), `docker.listContainers` (A6, app.js:1957), `backups.deleteSchedule` (A8, app.js:2121), `ownerWorkspace.getStatus` (A9, app.js:2009) |
| no reference in the renderer | 16 | `account.listDevices` (A1, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `account.revokeCurrentDevice` (A1, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `instances.evaluateRestartSchedules` (A3, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `marketplace.getImportSupport` (A4, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `marketplace.importCommunityTemplate` (A4, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `anxnet.renameDevice` (A5, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `nodes.credentialStatus` (A5, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `nodes.healthAll` (A5, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `docker.connectNetwork` (A6, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `docker.disconnectNetwork` (A6, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `connectivity.getDiagnostics` (A7, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `publicAccess.createFirewallRule` (A7, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `publicAccess.listServices` (A7, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `ssh.assignProfileToNode` (A8, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `ownerWorkspace.getFlags` (A9, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept), `ownerWorkspace.reorderPages` (A9, candidate unused by the UI; backend kept) |
